Built-in soft feet are shaped as heels. The sleeves end in plain cuffs, with bare hands showing. Quilted padding gives the shoulders, chest and thighs a gentle puffiness, with the outfit's details sewn on as raised fabric shapes. Skin shows at the face, hands and the open neckline, in __clothing_onesie_skin__. The whole suit looks like a plush stuffed-toy version of the uniform, made for an adult to lounge or party in. The suit is the whole Brazilian samba dancer outfit, not just a top, with a feathered bodysuit, a huge feather headdress (as the hood), heels (as the feet). It is shown in full, from the neckline down to the ankle cuffs. Novelty embellishments, holographic appliqué badges, mismatched sleeves and legs in different textures, padded 3D patch pockets and scalloped felt edging. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.
